# M8 Engineering composition and measured test cost

All 1362 native cases and 6 non-case checks passed in the complete selected population. All 1,313 M1 native identities remain reachable; no baseline cases were removed. The final population adds 16 M8 cases (15 Python and one Node), after the separately evidenced M2, M6 and M7 additions. Native discovery remains the execution owner; these generated reports are observations, not a test catalog.

The full run used the same explicit 13 Python entrypoints, Node suite, broad-smoke composition and four-worker budget as M2. Wall time was 559.484s; occupied worker time summed to 1913.045s. Baseline wall time was 538.762s with 1822.312s summed workers. Added cases and stronger fixtures change the work; single-run timing, cache and uncontrolled background load preclude a speedup claim. Worker time is not CPU time or wall time. No duration is invented for semantic judgments or conditional public operations.

Read [per-case costs](m8-case-costs.csv) for measured milliseconds and exact rerun commands, [full generated results](m8-validation-results.json) for execution status/scope, and [measurement basis](m8-validation-basis.json) for source identities, environment and complete population reconciliation. The two late M2 cases have no full-run baseline row; an empty CSV baseline cell means unavailable comparable observation, never zero cost.

Engineering and Validation retain the full source-to-product/check-selection/admission/discovery/isolation protection. No runner, fixture scheduling or discovery mechanism changes in M8 require a second full serial benchmark; fresh independent cases use owned roots and the unchanged native adapters. M2 establishes the runner's serial/parallel/report safety. Final row uniqueness, complete scope and M1/M2 identity preservation are checked explicitly.

Packaging now has literal independent normalization/count/ordering oracles, actual generated manifest and consumer-guide assertions, and real npm-packed empty/unsafe destination proof for both targets and modes. The compatible resolution of a discovered v1 producer/installer disagreement is approved DIST-DEC-07: new candidates use explicitly identified v2 UTF-8 byte ordering; existing v1 verification and all historical metadata stay intact. Fresh actual builders, current metadata, packed binary and Release candidates are tested together. Unknown/empty algorithms reject before consistency; public observation selects one declared digest without fallback. The legacy profile observer restores normalized, prefixed regular-file v1 evidence. Original ambiguous raw-case expectations are preserved as diagnostic history and are not claimed to be the identity-equal v2 regression.

Release covers all 16 allocated deterministic gaps through accepted same-tree controls, exact mutation/diagnostic and preservation assertions, counted process/network boundaries, genuine partial-write retry, bounded visibility, revoked approval and final public identity reread. The generated-region defect was fixed only after durable red proof; its frozen original test/helper pair passes with only production replaced. The four original provider regressions and both root v2 regression files likewise remain byte-identical across their recorded red/green runs. Earlier marker-only source observations are superseded for current integrated reliance by the complete final run; their original outcomes remain unchanged.

M3/M5 exact instructional subjects and independent semantic judgments remain scoped and applicable; fresh full adapters and current packed resources supply the new artifact observations. The independent scoped M8 review owns REL-MR-001/002/005 non-triggered applicability and future release/exception/incident duties. It does not mark absent live operations passed. Packaging's separately acknowledged tracked-support migration debt remains open; no tracked support reader or historical evidence is silently removed. No authored case JSON changed in M8, no external publication occurred, and this implementation handoff does not claim final review, Verify or PR readiness.

The bounded literal evidence below preserves author reports, exact commands, observed logs and deliberate private sensitivity mutations. Temporary paths identify actual runs; durable source subjects, literal fixtures and logs retain the reproduction basis. Supplied release-provider facts are controlled fixtures, not live registry or hosted authority.
